I've got it fixed.\n\nBy default, Flexbox containers try to stretch to fit their contents, even if the parent container is constrained to the height of the screen (`100vh`). Because of this, the chat window was expanding past the bottom of your screen and pushing the input bar down with it.\n\nI just added `minHeight: 0` to those flex containers, which forces them to respect the screen size boundaries. Now, the search bar and send button will stay permanently locked to the bottom of the screen, and only the white chat area will scroll when it fills up with messages!\n\nGo ahead and give it a test! You might just need to refresh your browser."}
